In the ideal case, the most likely measurement outcome directly corresponds to the binary expansion of the phase, while the probability of other outcomes decreases rapidly with the number of counting qubits. However, running deep QPE circuits on hardware presents challenges: the large number of qubits and entangling operations make the algorithm highly sensitive to decoherence and gate errors. This results in broadened and shifted distributions of bitstrings, masking the true eigenphase.
